133	pub fn init(args: &InitArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
134	    debug!("init: enter");
135	    let parent_dir = match &args.dir {
136	        Some(d) => d.clone(),
137	        None => std::env::current_dir()?,
138	    };
139	    let project_dir = parent_dir.join(&args.name);
140	    let session_dir = project_dir.join(".claude");
141	
142	    // Preflight checks
143	    info!("Preflight checks...");
144	
145	    // Check tools
146	    run("gh", &["auth", "status"], Path::new("."))
147	        .map_err(|_| "gh is not installed or not authenticated (run: gh auth login)")?;
148	    run("jj", &["--version"], Path::new(".")).map_err(|_| "jj is not installed")?;
149	
150	    let owner = match &args.owner {
151	        Some(o) => o.clone(),
152	        None => gh_whoami()?,
153	    };
154	
155	    if project_dir.exists() {
156	        return Err(format!("'{}' already exists", project_dir.display()).into());
157	    }
158	
159	    let code_repo = format!("{owner}/{}", args.name);
160	    let session_repo = format!("{owner}/{}.claude", args.name);
161	
162	    if gh_repo_exists(&owner, &args.name)? {
163	        return Err(format!("GitHub repo '{code_repo}' already exists").into());
164	    }
165	    if gh_repo_exists(&owner, &format!("{}.claude", args.name))? {
166	        return Err(format!("GitHub repo '{session_repo}' already exists").into());
167	    }
168	
169	    let visibility = if args.private {
170	        "--private"
171	    } else {
172	        "--public"
173	    };
174	
175	    if args.dry_run {
176	        info!("Dry run — would execute:");
177	        info!("  1. Create directories: {}", project_dir.display());
178	        info!("  2. git init + jj git init --colocate on both repos");
179	        info!("  3. Write .vc-config.toml and .gitignore to both");
180	        info!("  4. jj commit both with placeholder ochids");
181	        info!("  5. Get both chids, jj describe both with correct ochids");
182	        info!("  6. Remove jj from both (git clean -xdf)");
183	        info!("  7. gh repo create {session_repo} {visibility}, push");
184	        info!("  8. gh repo create {code_repo} {visibility}, push");
185	        info!("  9. jj git init --colocate on both repos");
186	        info!("  10. Create Claude Code symlink");
187	        return Ok(());
188	    }
189	
190	    // Step 1: Create directories
191	    info!("Step 1: Creating project directories...");
192	    mkdir_p(&project_dir)?;
193	    mkdir_p(&session_dir)?;
194	
195	    // Step 2: git init + jj init on both repos
196	    info!("Step 2: Initializing repos...");
197	    run("git", &["init"], &project_dir)?;
198	    run("jj", &["git", "init", "--colocate"], &project_dir)?;
199	    run("git", &["init"], &session_dir)?;
200	    run("jj", &["git", "init", "--colocate"], &session_dir)?;
201	
202	    // Step 3: Write config files
203	    info!("Step 3: Writing config files...");
204	    write_file(&project_dir.join(".vc-config.toml"), VC_CONFIG_CODE)?;
205	    write_file(&project_dir.join(".gitignore"), GITIGNORE_CODE)?;
206	    write_file(&session_dir.join(".vc-config.toml"), VC_CONFIG_SESSION)?;
207	    write_file(&session_dir.join(".gitignore"), GITIGNORE_SESSION)?;
208	
209	    // Step 4: jj commit both with placeholder ochids
210	    info!("Step 4: Committing both repos with placeholder ochids...");
211	    run(
212	        "jj",
213	        &["commit", "-m", "Initial commit\n\nochid: /none"],
214	        &project_dir,
215	    )?;
216	    run(
217	        "jj",
218	        &["commit", "-m", "Initial commit\n\nochid: /none"],
219	        &session_dir,
220	    )?;
221	
222	    // Step 5: Get both chids, then describe both with correct ochids
223	    info!("Step 5: Setting ochid cross-references...");
224	    let code_chid = jj_chid("@-", &project_dir)?;
225	    let session_chid = jj_chid("@-", &session_dir)?;
226	
227	    let code_desc = format!("Initial commit\n\nochid: /.claude/{session_chid}");
228	    let session_desc = format!("Initial commit\n\nochid: /{code_chid}");
229	    run("jj", &["describe", "@-", "-m", &code_desc], &project_dir)?;
230	    run("jj", &["describe", "@-", "-m", &session_desc], &session_dir)?;
231	
232	    {
233	        let hash = run("git", &["rev-parse", "HEAD"], &project_dir)?;
234	        debug!("code repo: chid={code_chid} hash={hash}");
235	        let hash = run("git", &["rev-parse", "HEAD"], &session_dir)?;
236	        debug!(".claude:   chid={session_chid} hash={hash}");
237	    }
238	
239	    // Step 6: Set bookmarks (creates git branches), then remove jj
240	    // Bookmarks must be set before removing .jj/ so git has a 'main' branch to push
241	    info!("Step 6: Setting bookmarks and removing jj...");
242	    run("jj", &["bookmark", "set", "main", "-r", "@-"], &project_dir)?;
243	    run("jj", &["bookmark", "set", "main", "-r", "@-"], &session_dir)?;
244	    // Clean .claude first, then code repo with --exclude to preserve .claude/
245	    run("git", &["clean", "-xdf"], &session_dir)?;
246	    run(
247	        "git",
248	        &["clean", "-xdf", "--exclude", ".claude"],
249	        &project_dir,
250	    )?;
251	    // After removing .jj/, git HEAD is detached — reattach to main
252	    run("git", &["checkout", "main"], &session_dir)?;
253	    run("git", &["checkout", "main"], &project_dir)?;
254	
255	    // Step 7: Create .claude GitHub repo and push
256	    let session_url = format!("git@github.com:{session_repo}.git");
257	    info!("Step 7: Creating GitHub repo {session_repo}...");
258	    run(
259	        "gh",
260	        &["repo", "create", &session_repo, visibility],
261	        &project_dir,
262	    )?;
263	    run(
264	        "git",
265	        &["remote", "add", "origin", &session_url],
266	        &session_dir,
267	    )?;
268	    run_retry(
269	        "git",
270	        &["push", "-u", "origin", "main"],
271	        &session_dir,
272	        args.push_retries,
273	        args.push_retry_delay,
274	    )?;
275	
276	    // Step 8: Create code GitHub repo and push
277	    let code_url = format!("git@github.com:{code_repo}.git");
278	    info!("Step 8: Creating GitHub repo {code_repo}...");
279	    run(
280	        "gh",
281	        &["repo", "create", &code_repo, visibility],
282	        &project_dir,
283	    )?;
284	    run("git", &["remote", "add", "origin", &code_url], &project_dir)?;
285	    run_retry(
286	        "git",
287	        &["push", "-u", "origin", "main"],
288	        &project_dir,
289	        args.push_retries,
290	        args.push_retry_delay,
291	    )?;
292	
293	    // Step 9: Re-initialize jj on both repos
294	    info!("Step 9: Re-initializing jj on both repos...");
295	    run("jj", &["git", "init", "--colocate"], &project_dir)?;
296	    run("jj", &["bookmark", "set", "main", "-r", "@-"], &project_dir)?;
297	    run("jj", &["bookmark", "track", "main@origin"], &project_dir)?;
298	    let code_chid_final = jj_chid("@-", &project_dir)?;
299	
300	    run("jj", &["git", "init", "--colocate"], &session_dir)?;
301	    run("jj", &["bookmark", "set", "main", "-r", "@-"], &session_dir)?;
302	    run("jj", &["bookmark", "track", "main@origin"], &session_dir)?;
303	    let session_chid_final = jj_chid("@-", &session_dir)?;
304	
305	    // Step 10: Create Claude Code symlink
306	    info!("Step 10: Creating Claude Code symlink...");
307	    let symlink_dir = {
308	        let home =
309	            std::env::var("HOME").map_err(|_| "HOME environment variable not set".to_string())?;
310	        PathBuf::from(home).join(".claude").join("projects")
311	    };
312	
313	    let sl = symlink::SymLink::new(&project_dir, Path::new(".claude"), &symlink_dir)?;
314	    sl.create(false)?;
315	
316	    info!("");
317	    info!("Done! Project created at {}", project_dir.display());
318	    info!("  Code repo:    {code_repo}  (chid={code_chid_final})");
319	    info!("  Session repo: {session_repo}  (chid={session_chid_final})");
320	    info!(
321	        "  Symlink:      {} -> {}",
322	        sl.symlink_path.display(),
323	        sl.abs_target.display()
324	    );
325	
326	    debug!("init: exit");
327	    Ok(())
328	}

66	        Status labels:\n  \
67	          ok    — ochid trailer is valid (no change)\n  \
68	          fix   — ochid has issues, shows proposed fix (dry-run)\n  \
69	          fixed — ochid was rewritten (--no-dry-run)\n  \
70	          add   — missing ochid, match found, shows proposed addition (dry-run)\n  \
71	          added — missing ochid was added (--no-dry-run)\n  \
72	          skip  — skipped (no ochid, no match, or max-fixes reached)\n  \
73	          err   — ID not found and no --fallback provided")]
74	    FixDesc(fix_desc::FixDescArgs),

84

87	        Designed for the bot to atomically finalize its session repo:\n\
88	        --detach exits immediately, --delay waits for trailing writes,\n\
89	        --squash folds them in, --bookmark + --push sends it upstream.\n\
90	        Every flag is opt-in. See README.md for details.")]
91	    Finalize(finalize::FinalizeArgs),
92	}

165	- **Bookmark is tracking its remote** (if `--push`): scan
166	  `jj bookmark list -a <name>` output. Tracked remotes appear indented
167	  (`  @origin: …`); a non-tracked remote appears at column 0 as
168	  `<bookmark>@<remote>: …`. If found, error with a remediation hint:
169	  `run `jj bookmark track <bookmark>@<remote> -R <repo>` to fix`.
170	
171	`find_non_tracking_remote()` is a pure helper tested in unit tests
172	against a few representative `jj bookmark list -a` output snippets.
173	
174	Bookmark-existence is still re-checked inside `finalize_exec()` as
175	defense-in-depth — pre-flight catches it synchronously, the child
176	catches any unlikely race between pre-flight and execution.
177	
178	### 0.33.0-dev1.1 — repo-state checks + plan logging + post-commit workflow
179	
180	Two findings after dev1 landed:
181	
182	1. After `jj commit -R .`, the app repo's `main` bookmark did not
183	   advance to `@-` (the `.claude` repo's config does advance it, the
184	   app repo's does not). Easy to miss — the `Parent commit (@-)`
185	   line in `jj commit` output shows `<bookmark>* |` when the bookmark
186	   sits on that commit; when absent, the bookmark is stuck behind.
187	2. `finalize` pre-flight should also sanity-check the target repo
188	   state before detaching, and should *log the plan* so the user
189	   knows what's about to happen.
190

192

  "old_string": "### 0.33.0-dev2 — subprocess output visibility in `common::run()`\n\n`common::run()` previously demoted both captured stdout and stderr to\n`debug!`, so without `-v` the user saw nothing from invoked subprocesses\non success. jj's human-readable messages (`Moved 1 bookmarks to …`,\n`Rebased N commits`, `Nothing changed.`, push summaries) go to **stderr**,\nwhile data output (bookmark lists, commit IDs) goes to **stdout**. The\nfix follows that split:\n\n- **stderr at `info!` on success** — the user sees what jj actually did.\n- **stdout at `debug!`** — callers consume it as data; `info!` would\n  flood the user with output they didn't ask for (bookmark lists,\n  revset queries).\n- **Failure path unchanged** — `run()` returns `Err` with stderr in the\n  message; `main::run_command` already logs it at `error!`.\n\nNo new tests: the behavior is in the shape of a log call (routing via\nthe `log` crate), the logger already has unit tests, and the subprocess\nside is exercised end-to-end during `vc-x1 finalize` retests. Adding a\nunit test here would require mocking `std::process::Command`, which is\nmore noise than signal.",
  "new_string": "### 0.33.0-dev2 — subprocess visibility + `test-fixture` subcommand\n\nTwo things land together:\n\n1. **`common::run()` subprocess output visibility.** Previously both\n   captured stdout and stderr were demoted to `debug!`, so without `-v`\n   the user saw nothing from invoked subprocesses on success. jj prints\n   human-readable messages (`Moved 1 bookmarks to …`, `Rebased N\n   commits`, `Nothing changed.`, push summaries) to **stderr**, while\n   data output (bookmark lists, commit IDs) goes to **stdout**. Split\n   accordingly:\n   - **stderr at `info!` on success** — user sees what jj actually did.\n   - **stdout at `debug!`** — callers consume it as data; `info!` would\n     flood the user with bookmark lists and revset results.\n   - Failure path unchanged — `run()` returns `Err` carrying stderr;\n     `main::run_command` already logs it at `error!`.\n\n2. **`vc-x1 test-fixture` subcommand** — scaffolds a throwaway jj repo\n   and local bare-git remote under `$TMPDIR/vc-x1-test-<timestamp>/`\n   (or `--path PATH`). Output:\n   ```\n   <base>/\n     remote.git/   bare git remote (origin)\n     work/        jj repo, colocated, main bookmark tracks origin/main\n   ```\n   Retests for finalize (dev3, dev4, and beyond) point at\n   `--repo <base>/work` instead of the live `.claude` or app repo.\n   Two prior retests during dev1 and dev2 accidentally ran finalize\n   against `.claude` with no `--detach`, squashing + pushing the live\n   session repo mid-stream — that's the motivation.\n\nNo unit tests for the subprocess-logging change: it's one `info!`\ncall; mocking `std::process::Command` is more noise than signal. The\nlogger already has unit tests for routing. `test-fixture` gets arg\nparsing tests; its filesystem side belongs to future integration tests\n(see the todo entry for `tests/` with `tempfile`)."
}

1	# vc-x1
2

18	
19	This is experiment 1 to explore creating a Vibe Coding (vc) environment.
20	We will investigate ways of using the dual jj-git repo concept, explored
21	in [hw-jjg-bot](https://github.com/winksaville/hw-jjg-bot.git) to
22	initially make it easy to see how the code base evolved. This is
23	made possible by the fact that we have two repos one with the code
24	and one with the conversation with the bot.
25	
26	I've chosen the jj-git environment because jj provides the concept that
27	each commit has an immutable changeID as well as the mutable commitID
28	of git. The idea is that each commit made on repo A writes the
29	changeID in the commit message to repo B. Thus there is a cross reference
30	between the two repos and this will allow vc-x1 to show how the repo
31	evolved and the entity (bot or human) can more clearly understand **how** and
32	most importantly **why** the code evolved.
33	
34	The solution space is wide open, from trivial CLI, web or app based
35	(mobile/non-mobile). In addition, I could see this as an extension to
36	existing programming editors like vscode and zed or even creating our
37	own IDE for vc.
38	
39	See [Initial commit with dual jj-git repos](./notes/chores-01.md#initial-commit-with-dual-jj-git-repos)
40	for how the initial commit was created with the dual jj-git repos. After
41	doing so and I then created this README.md file.
42	
43	## Usage
44	
45	```
46	vc-x1 list [-r REVISION] [-n COMMITS]  # List commits in a jj repo
47	vc-x1 desc [-r REVISION] [-n COMMITS]  # Show full description of a commit
48	vc-x1 chid [-r REVISION] [-n COMMITS]  # Print changeID(s) for a revision
49	vc-x1 show [-r REVISION] [-n COMMITS]  # Show commit details and diff summary
50	vc-x1 validate-desc [OPTS]                 # Validate commit descriptions
51	vc-x1 fix-desc [OPTS]                     # Fix commit descriptions (dry-run default)
52	vc-x1 clone <REPO> [NAME] [OPTS]          # Clone a dual-repo project
53	vc-x1 init <NAME> [OPTS]                  # Create a new dual-repo project
54	vc-x1 symlink [TARGET] [OPTS]             # Create Claude Code project symlink
55	vc-x1 finalize --bookmark <B> [OPTS]       # Squash working copy into target
56	vc-x1 --version                            # Print version
57	vc-x1 --help                           # Print help
58	```
59	
60	**The `..` notation:** dots on the revision show which direction to
61	list commits:
62	
63	- `x..` — x at top, ancestors below (older commits)
64	- `..x` — descendants above (newer commits), x at bottom
65	- `..x..` — both directions, x in the middle
66	
67	COMMITS is the total number of commits to show (x is always included).
68	Without dots, `x` with a count defaults to `x..` (ancestors).
69	For `..x..`, the budget is split: ancestors get the extra on odd counts.
70	
```

27-each commit has an immutable changeID as well as the mutable commitID
28-of git. The idea is that each commit made on repo A writes the
29-changeID in the commit message to repo B. Thus there is a cross reference
30-between the two repos and this will allow vc-x1 to show how the repo
31-evolved and the entity (bot or human) can more clearly understand **how** and
32-most importantly **why** the code evolved.
33-
34-The solution space is wide open, from trivial CLI, web or app based
35-(mobile/non-mobile). In addition, I could see this as an extension to
36-existing programming editors like vscode and zed or even creating our
37-own IDE for vc.
38-
39-See [Initial commit with dual jj-git repos](./notes/chores-01.md#initial-commit-with-dual-jj-git-repos)
40-for how the initial commit was created with the dual jj-git repos. After
41-doing so and I then created this README.md file.
42-
43-## Usage
--
405:### Testing finalize
406-
407-Always test against a throwaway jj repo, never the live workspace.
408-Use `mktemp -d` for unique directories so results can't be confused
409-with a previous run:
410-
411-```bash
412-# Create and init a temp repo
413-dir=$(mktemp -d /tmp/vc-x1-test-XXXXXX)
414-(cd "$dir" && jj git init)
415-
416-# Foreground (default, blocks, logs to test dir)
417-vc-x1 finalize --repo "$dir" --bookmark main --log "$dir/finalize.log"
418-cat "$dir/finalize.log"
419-
420-# Detached (returns immediately, child logs to test dir)
421-dir2=$(mktemp -d /tmp/vc-x1-test-XXXXXX)
422-(cd "$dir2" && jj git init)
423-vc-x1 finalize --detach --repo "$dir2" --bookmark main --log "$dir2/finalize.log"
424-cat "$dir2/finalize.log"
425-```
426-
427-The log file shows timestamped (nanoseconds) entries with PIDs, covering
428-the full flow: `main` entry/exit, `finalize` entry/exit, `detach`
429-spawn, and `finalize_exec` in the child process.
430-
431-## Cross-repo Linking with Git Trailers
432-
433-Commits in each repo use [git trailers](https://git-scm.com/docs/git-interpret-trailers)
434-to cross-reference their counterpart in the other repo. The `ochid`
435-(Other Change ID) trailer contains a workspace-root-relative path
```

  "old_string": "### Testing finalize\n\nAlways test against a throwaway jj repo, never the live workspace.\nUse `mktemp -d` for unique directories so results can't be confused\nwith a previous run:\n\n```bash\n# Create and init a temp repo\ndir=$(mktemp -d /tmp/vc-x1-test-XXXXXX)\n(cd \"$dir\" && jj git init)\n\n# Foreground (default, blocks, logs to test dir)\nvc-x1 finalize --repo \"$dir\" --bookmark main --log \"$dir/finalize.log\"\ncat \"$dir/finalize.log\"\n\n# Detached (returns immediately, child logs to test dir)\ndir2=$(mktemp -d /tmp/vc-x1-test-XXXXXX)\n(cd \"$dir2\" && jj git init)\nvc-x1 finalize --detach --repo \"$dir2\" --bookmark main --log \"$dir2/finalize.log\"\ncat \"$dir2/finalize.log\"\n```\n\nThe log file shows timestamped (nanoseconds) entries with PIDs, covering\nthe full flow: `main` entry/exit, `finalize` entry/exit, `detach`\nspawn, and `finalize_exec` in the child process.",
  "new_string": "### test-fixture\n\nScaffold a throwaway jj repo and local bare-git remote for testing\n`finalize` (and other subcommands) without touching live workspace\nrepos. Produces a tracked `main` bookmark on a described commit so\n`--push` flows work end-to-end.\n\n```bash\nvc-x1 test-fixture                 # base = $TMPDIR/vc-x1-test-<timestamp>\nvc-x1 test-fixture --path /tmp/t1  # explicit path\n```\n\nLayout:\n```\n<base>/\n  remote.git/   bare git remote (origin)\n  work/        jj repo, colocated, main tracks origin/main\n```\n\n### Testing finalize\n\nAlways test against a throwaway fixture, never the live workspace.\nUse `test-fixture` (above) to scaffold one per test:\n\n```bash\n# Create fixture and capture the work-repo path\nbase=$(mktemp -u /tmp/vc-x1-test-XXXXXX)\nvc-x1 test-fixture --path \"$base\"\nwork=\"$base/work\"\n\n# Foreground (default, blocks, log to test dir)\nvc-x1 finalize --repo \"$work\" --push main --log \"$work/finalize.log\"\ncat \"$work/finalize.log\"\n\n# Detached (returns immediately, child logs to test dir)\nbase2=$(mktemp -u /tmp/vc-x1-test-XXXXXX)\nvc-x1 test-fixture --path \"$base2\"\nwork2=\"$base2/work\"\nvc-x1 finalize --detach --repo \"$work2\" --push main --log \"$work2/finalize.log\"\ncat \"$work2/finalize.log\"\n```\n\nThe log file shows timestamped (nanoseconds) entries with PIDs, covering\nthe full flow: `main` entry/exit, `finalize` entry/exit, `detach`\nspawn, and `finalize_exec` in the child process."
}

"feat: subprocess visibility + test-fixture subcommand (0.33.0-dev2)" \
-m "Two things:

common::run() subprocess output — previously demoted stdout and
stderr to debug!, so without -v the user saw nothing from invoked
subprocesses on success. Split by jj's stream convention:
- stderr → info! on success (human-readable messages)
- stdout → debug! (data output callers consume)
- failure path unchanged (Err carries stderr for error!-level surface)

vc-x1 test-fixture subcommand — scaffolds a throwaway jj repo +
local bare-git remote under \$TMPDIR/vc-x1-test-<timestamp>/ (or
--path PATH). Produces a tracked 'main' bookmark on a described
commit so --push flows work end-to-end. Use for finalize retests
instead of the live .claude/app repos (prior retests accidentally
squashed+pushed live state). README.md 'Testing finalize' section
updated to use the new subcommand.

ochid: /.claude/okynxqnvtsky" \
-R .

```rust
info!("Step 2: Initializing work repo (git + jj colocated)...");
run("git", &["init"], &work_dir)?;

Both repos carry\n/// `.vc-config.toml`, `.gitignore`, an initial described commit with\n/// matching `ochid:` trailers, and a tracked `main` bookmark.\n#[derive(Args, Debug)]\npub struct TestFixtureArgs {\n    /// Fixture root directory [default: $TMPDIR/vc-x1-test-<timestamp>]\n    #[arg(long, value_name = \"PATH\")]\n    pub path: Option<PathBuf>,\n}\n\nconst VC_CONFIG_CODE: &str = r#\"# vc-config: Vibe Coding workspace configuration\n[workspace]\npath = \"/\"\nother-repo = \".claude\"\n\"#;\n\nconst VC_CONFIG_SESSION: &str = r#\"# vc-config: Vibe Coding workspace configuration\n[workspace]\npath = \"/.claude\"\nother-repo = \"..\"\n\"#;\n\nconst GITIGNORE_CODE: &str = \"/target\n/.claude\n/.git\n/.jj\n\";\n\nconst GITIGNORE_SESSION: &str = \".git\n.jj\n\";\n\nfn jj_chid(rev: &str, cwd: &Path) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let full = run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\n            \"log\", \"-r\", rev, \"--no-graph\", \"-T\", \"change_id\", \"--limit\", \"1\",\n        ],\n        cwd,\n    )?;\n    Ok(full[..full.len().min(12)].to_string())\n}\n\npub fn test_fixture(args: &TestFixtureArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    debug!(\"test_fixture: enter\");\n\n    let base = match &args.path {\n        Some(p) => p.clone(),\n        None => {\n            let ts = SystemTime::now()\n                .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)\n                .map_err(|e| format!(\"time error: {e}\"))?\n                .as_nanos();\n            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(\"vc-x1-test-{ts}\"))\n        }\n    };\n\n    if base.exists() {\n        return Err(format!(\"'{}' already exists\", base.display()).into());\n    }\n\n    info!(\"Creating test fixture at {}\", base.display());\n\n    let remote_code = base.join(\"remote-code.git\");\n    let remote_claude = base.join(\"remote-claude.git\");\n    let work_dir = base.join(\"work\");\n    let session_dir = work_dir.join(\".claude\");\n\n    mkdir_p(&base)?;\n    mkdir_p(&remote_code)?;\n    mkdir_p(&remote_claude)?;\n    mkdir_p(&work_dir)?;\n    mkdir_p(&session_dir)?;\n\n    info!(\"Step 1: Initializing bare git remotes...\");\n    run(\"git\", &[\"init\", \"--bare\"], &remote_code)?;\n    run(\"git\", &[\"init\", \"--bare\"], &remote_claude)?;\n\n    info!(\"Step 2: Initializing work repo (jj colocated)...\");\n    run(\"jj\", &[\"git\", \"init\", \"--colocate\"], &work_dir)?;\n    write_file(&work_dir.join(\".vc-config.toml\"), VC_CONFIG_CODE)?;\n    write_file(&work_dir.join(\".gitignore\"), GITIGNORE_CODE)?;\n\n    info!(\"Step 3: Initializing .claude session repo (jj colocated)...\");\n    run(\"jj\", &[\"git\", \"init\", \"--colocate\"], &session_dir)?;\n    write_file(&session_dir.join(\".vc-config.toml\"), VC_CONFIG_SESSION)?;\n   "old_string": "### test-fixture\n\nScaffold a throwaway jj repo and local bare-git remote for testing\n`finalize` (and other subcommands) without touching live workspace\nrepos. Produces a tracked `main` bookmark on a described commit so\n`--push` flows work end-to-end.\n\n```bash\nvc-x1 test-fixture                 # base = $TMPDIR/vc-x1-test-<timestamp>\nvc-x1 test-fixture --path /tmp/t1  # explicit path\n```\n\nLayout:\n```\n<base>/\n  remote.git/   bare git remote (origin)\n  work/        jj repo, colocated, main tracks origin/main\n```\n\n### Testing finalize\n\nAlways test against a throwaway fixture, never the live workspace.\nUse `test-fixture` (above) to scaffold one per test:\n\n```bash\n# Create fixture and capture the work-repo path\nbase=$(mktemp -u /tmp/vc-x1-test-XXXXXX)\nvc-x1 test-fixture --path \"$base\"\nwork=\"$base/work\"\n\n# Foreground (default, blocks, log to test dir)\nvc-x1 finalize --repo \"$work\" --push main --log \"$work/finalize.log\"\ncat \"$work/finalize.log\"\n\n# Detached (returns immediately, child logs to test dir)\nbase2=$(mktemp -u /tmp/vc-x1-test-XXXXXX)\nvc-x1 test-fixture --path \"$base2\"\nwork2=\"$base2/work\"\nvc-x1 finalize --detach --repo \"$work2\" --push main --log \"$work2/finalize.log\"\ncat \"$work2/finalize.log\"\n```",
  "new_string": "### test-fixture\n\nScaffold a throwaway dual-repo jj workspace + local bare-git remotes\nfor testing `finalize` (and other subcommands) without touching live\nworkspace repos. Mirrors the real `vc-x1 init` layout minus the GitHub\nside and the `~/.claude/projects/` symlink. Both repos get a described\ninitial commit with matching `ochid:` trailers, a tracked `main`\nbookmark, and a pushed remote — so `finalize --push` flows work\nend-to-end on either side.\n\n```bash\nvc-x1 test-fixture                 # base = $TMPDIR/vc-x1-test-<timestamp>\nvc-x1 test-fixture --path /tmp/t1  # explicit path\n```\n\nLayout:\n```\n<base>/\n  remote-code.git/     bare git remote for code repo\n  remote-claude.git/   bare git remote for .claude session repo\n  work/                code repo (jj colocated, main tracks origin)\n    .vc-config.toml    path=\"/\",       other-repo=\".claude\"\n    .gitignore         /.claude /.git /.jj /target\n    .claude/           session repo (jj colocated, main tracks origin)\n      .vc-config.toml  path=\"/.claude\", other-repo=\"..\"\n      .gitignore       .git .jj\n```\n\n### Testing finalize\n\nAlways test against a throwaway fixture, never the live workspace.\nUse `test-fixture` (above) to scaffold one per test:\n\n```bash\n# Create fixture and capture paths\nbase=$(mktemp -u /tmp/vc-x1-test-XXXXXX)\nvc-x1 test-fixture --path \"$base\"\nwork=\"$base/work\"\nsession=\"$base/work/.claude\"\n\n# Foreground (default, blocks, log to fixture)\nvc-x1 finalize --repo \"$work\" --push main --log \"$work/finalize.log\"\ncat \"$work/finalize.log\"\n\n# Detached (returns immediately, child logs to fixture)\nbase2=$(mktemp -u /tmp/vc-x1-test-XXXXXX)\nvc-x1 test-fixture --path \"$base2\"\nsession2=\"$base2/work/.claude\"\nvc-x1 finalize --detach --repo \"$session2\" --squash --push main \\\n    --log \"$session2/finalize.log\"\ncat \"$session2/finalize.log\"\n```"
}

Output:\n   ```\n   <base>/\n     remote.git/   bare git remote (origin)\n     work/        jj repo, colocated, main bookmark tracks origin/main\n   ```\n   Retests for finalize (dev3, dev4, and beyond) point at\n   `--repo <base>/work` instead of the live `.claude` or app repo.\n   Two prior retests during dev1 and dev2 accidentally ran finalize\n   against `.claude` with no `--detach`, squashing + pushing the live\n   session repo mid-stream — that's the motivation.",
  "new_string": "2. **`vc-x1 test-fixture` subcommand** — scaffolds a throwaway dual-repo\n   workspace under `$TMPDIR/vc-x1-test-<timestamp>/` (or `--path PATH`).\n   Mirrors the real `vc-x1 init` layout minus GitHub and minus the\n   `~/.claude/projects/` symlink. Both repos get a described initial\n   commit with matching `ochid:` trailers, a tracked `main` bookmark,\n   and a pushed local bare-git remote — so `finalize --push` works\n   end-to-end on either side.\n   ```\n   <base>/\n     remote-code.git/     bare remote for code repo\n     remote-claude.git/   bare remote for .claude repo\n     work/                code repo (jj colocated, main tracks origin)\n       .vc-config.toml    path=\"/\",        other-repo=\".claude\"\n       .gitignore         /.claude /.git /.jj /target\n       .claude/           session repo (jj colocated, main tracks origin)\n         .vc-config.toml  path=\"/.claude\", other-repo=\"..\"\n         .gitignore       .git .jj\n   ```\n   Retests for finalize (dev3, dev4, and beyond) point at\n   `--repo <base>/work` or `--repo <base>/work/.claude` instead of the\n   live workspace. Two prior retests during dev1 and dev2 accidentally\n   ran finalize against the live `.claude` with no `--detach`,\n   squashing + pushing mid-stream — that's the motivation.\n\n   Step order matters: create outer work repo → write its `.gitignore`\n   excluding `.claude` → only then init `.claude/` as a separate repo,\n   so the outer jj doesn't snapshot the nested `.jj/.git` before the\n   ignore rule is in place."
}

workspace under \$TMPDIR/vc-x1-test-<timestamp>/ (or --path PATH).

work/.claude/, each with .vc-config.toml, .gitignore, a described
initial commit carrying matching ochid: trailers, and a tracked
main bookmark pushed to a local bare-git remote. Finalize retests
point at --repo work/ or --repo work/.claude/ instead of live
repos (prior retests squashed+pushed live .claude mid-session).
README.md's Testing finalize section now uses it.

ochid: /.claude/okynxqnvtsky" \
-R .

> DEVELOPER

I think we should push + finalize at each -dev step both repos so we don't loose them, but also as .claude isn't finalized so it's "missing" some commits.

> AGENT

Good point on both counts. Current unpushed state:
- **App repo**: `main` at dev2, **3 commits ahead of origin** (dev1, dev1.1, dev2)
- **.claude**: `main` at dev2, **1 commit ahead of origin** (origin has dev1.1-squashed, local has dev2 + trailing session writes)

**Proposed catch-up now:**
1. `jj git push --bookmark main -R .` — push app repo (dev1 + dev1.1 + dev2)
2. `vc-x1 finalize --repo .claude --squash --push main --delay 0` — synchronous (no `--detach`) so I can approve + see output. `--delay 0` since we're not racing session-end. Squashes current trailing writes into dev2, pushes main.

**Proposed per-dev workflow going forward** (to codify in CLAUDE.md):

After each dev commit pair:
1. `jj git push --bookmark main -R .`
2. `vc-x1 finalize --repo .claude --squash --push main --delay 0`

Rationale: avoids losing work, keeps remote in sync, squashes trailing session writes into the current dev commit. Session-end finalize stays as-is (with `--detach --delay 10`) to catch the truly-final trailing writes.

Alternative framing: **just push** per dev (no squash), and only finalize at session end. Simpler, but dev commits in `.claude` miss trailing writes.

Which do you prefer — push+finalize per dev, or push-only per dev? And should I do the catch-up now?

> DEVELOPER

push+finalize per dev it keeps things in sync

And I'll fold the CLAUDE.md workflow update into the next dev commit (dev3).

Approve both commands?

the finalize needs to be --detach --delay 10 so we catch everything. I'll need to tell you to continue, but that's a good thing as a -devX step is "important" and really no different than a single commit.

Then I stop and wait for "continue" before dev3.

  "old_string": "    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new(exe);\n    cmd.args(&args).stdin(Stdio::null());\n\n    #[cfg(unix)]\n    {\n        use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;\n        unsafe {\n            cmd.pre_exec(|| {\n                libc::setsid();\n                Ok(())\n            });\n        }\n    }",
  "new_string": "    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new(exe);\n    cmd.args(&args).stdin(Stdio::null());\n\n    // Hand the child the user's controlling terminal when one exists so\n    // its output stays visible after the parent exits. Inherited pipes\n    // are unreliable here — the bash-tool / cron caller typically closes\n    // its read end on parent exit, silently dropping child writes. Fall\n    // back to null when there is no tty (pipe-invoked / CI / Windows);\n    // the log file is authoritative in that case.\n    match std::fs::OpenOptions::new()\n        .read(true)\n        .write(true)\n        .open(\"/dev/tty\")\n    {\n        Ok(tty) => {\n            debug!(\"detach: /dev/tty opened, child output → terminal\");\n            let out = tty.try_clone()?;\n            cmd.stdout(Stdio::from(out));\n            cmd.stderr(Stdio::from(tty));\n        }\n        Err(e) => {\n            debug!(\"detach: /dev/tty unavailable ({e}), child output → null\");\n            cmd.stdout(Stdio::null());\n            cmd.stderr(Stdio::null());\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[cfg(unix)]\n    {\n        use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;\n        unsafe {\n            cmd.pre_exec(|| {\n                libc::setsid();\n                Ok(())\n            });\n        }\n    }"
}

The\nlogger already has unit tests for routing. `test-fixture` gets arg\nparsing tests; its filesystem side belongs to future integration tests\n(see the todo entry for `tests/` with `tempfile`).",
  "new_string": "No unit tests for the subprocess-logging change: it's one `info!`\ncall; mocking `std::process::Command` is more noise than signal. The\nlogger already has unit tests for routing. `test-fixture` gets arg\nparsing tests; its filesystem side belongs to future integration tests\n(see the todo entry for `tests/` with `tempfile`).\n\n### 0.33.0-dev3 — `/dev/tty` reconnect + per-dev push+finalize\n\n**`detach()` now explicitly wires the child's stdout/stderr** rather\nthan inheriting them. Previously only `stdin` was nulled; stdout/stderr\ninherited from the parent, which meant:\n\n- In a real terminal, the child's writes happened to still reach the\n  shell — but that was accidental, not guaranteed.\n- Via a pipe-invoked caller (Claude Code's Bash tool, cron, CI), the\n  bash tool closed its read end of the inherited pipe the moment the\n  parent exited, so the child's subsequent writes either hit SIGPIPE\n  or vanished.\n\nNew behavior, in `detach()` before `cmd.spawn()`:\n\n- Open `/dev/tty` read+write. On Unix with a controlling terminal,\n  this gives a fd that stays valid regardless of what the parent's\n  stdout/stderr pointed at (and regardless of pipe closures on parent\n  exit).\n- If `/dev/tty` opens, clone the fd and attach as the child's stdout\n  and stderr. User sees child output in the shell they invoked from.\n- If it doesn't open (pipe-invoked, no controlling terminal, Windows),\n  fall back to `Stdio::null()`. The log file is authoritative in that\n  case. This is the Claude-Code-Bash-tool path.\n\nNo new tests: the behavior branches on runtime environment\n(`/dev/tty` presence), which unit tests can't meaningfully exercise\nwithout a tty fixture. Verified end-to-end via `test-fixture` — the\ndetached child's output reaches the log file in the pipe-invoked\ncase, and reaches the terminal in an interactive shell.\n\n**CLAUDE.md: per-dev push+finalize workflow.** New \"Per-dev step\nworkflow\" subsection explains that each `-devN` step is treated like\na mini session-end: push both repos and finalize `.claude` with\n`--detach --delay 10`, then wait for the user to say \"continue\".\nMatches the discipline the user called out — a `-devN` commit is\nimportant, same as any single commit being shipped to remote."
}

jj commit -m \
"feat: detached /dev/tty reconnect + per-dev workflow + README examples (0.33.0-dev3)" \
-m "finalize::detach() now explicitly wires the child's stdout/stderr
instead of inheriting from the parent. Previously only stdin was
nulled; inherited pipes to the bash-tool caller got closed on
parent exit, silently dropping child writes.

- Open /dev/tty read+write in the parent. If available, pass clones
  as the child's stdout and stderr — output stays visible in the
  user's shell after the parent exits.
- Falls back to Stdio::null() when no controlling terminal
  (pipe-invoked caller, cron, Windows). Log file is authoritative.

gets push + finalize (--detach --delay 10), then wait for the user
to say 'continue'. A -devN commit shipped to remote is 'important'
in its own right, so same discipline as session-end.

test-fixture, what the parent prints during a detached finalize,

> AGENT

Good catches, and good news: the preflight doing its job is exactly what we wanted. A couple of observations:

**The "hang"**: That wasn't actually a hang. Look at this line from your output:
```
$ Working copy  (@) now at: xptrqwlq 281d96c3 (empty) (no description set)
```
The `$` is your shell prompt. The child's output was delayed by `--delay` and landed AFTER your shell had already returned and printed the next prompt — so the child's text printed on the same visual line as `$`. Pressing Enter just redrew the prompt. This is the `/dev/tty` reconnect from dev3 *working correctly* — the child's output reached your terminal. Without dev3, the text would have been lost entirely.

**The `--push main` failure on `work/`**: Correct preflight behavior. `--push` without `--squash` targets `@` (the empty working copy), which has no description → push would fail. `--push --squash` targets `@-` (the described initial commit) → works. So the suggestion I printed (`--push main --detach` without `--squash`) is a footgun right after a fresh fixture — preflight catches it but the user bumps into a pointless error.

**Proposed refinements** (fold into dev3):

1. **Fix test-fixture's "Try:" output** — both suggestions should use `--squash`:
   ```
   vc-x1 finalize --repo <work> --squash --push main --detach
   vc-x1 finalize --repo <work>/.claude --squash --push main --detach
   ```
2. **README note** — explain that a detached child's output can appear on top of your next shell prompt (the `/dev/tty` reconnect is working; press Enter to redraw).

**On "`vc-x1 init` inside test-fixture"**: init adds GitHub repo creation + `~/.claude/projects/` symlink + SSH push — not what you want in a throwaway fixture. The two commands already do very similar local scaffolding and could share a helper (refactor), but that's architectural cleanup, not a correctness fix. I'd defer to its own todo.

**On "refine for committing"**: we could optionally have `test-fixture --with-staged-commit` that leaves some described-but-uncommitted state in `@`, mimicking mid-session. But after playing with the current fixture I think the user just needs `--squash` in the hint.

Shall I apply these two fixes and file the init/test-fixture consolidation as a todo?

> AGENT

Let me unpack a few things first, then discuss the refinements:

**On "Nothing changed" vs `main*` — push actually did happen.** Timeline during that detached run:

1. **Squash**: `Working copy (@) now at: ... Parent commit (@-) : ... main* | initial commit` — at this moment, post-squash, `main*` means "local has changes not yet on remote", which is accurate *right then*.
2. **Bookmark set**: `Nothing changed.` — this is the `jj bookmark set main -r @-` output, which is a no-op because `jj squash` automatically carried `main` along when `@-` got rewritten. Not related to push.
3. **Push**: `Changes to push to origin: Move sideways bookmark main from a8d1c04c0bb5 to bef6f6739e2c` — this IS the successful push. "Sideways" because squash rewrote `@-` (different commit ID, same position, hence not fast-forward).

Your later `jj show @- -R .claude` confirms it: `Bookmarks: main main@git main@origin` — `main@origin` is present, push succeeded.

**That said, the interleaved jj output is confusing.** We could print an info! marker before each subprocess so output is readable:
```
finalize: jj squash ...
  [squash output]
finalize: jj bookmark set ...
  Nothing changed.
finalize: jj git push ...
  Changes to push to origin: ...
```
Small improvement; worth doing.

**Agreed refinements:**

1. **"Try:" output** — drop the `work/` finalize (we never finalize the code repo; it's always `jj git push`). Print:
   ```
   Try:
     # code repo: plain push
     jj git push -R <base>/work
     # session repo: finalize (squash + push)
     vc-x1 finalize --repo <base>/work/.claude --squash --push main --detach
   ```

2. **Simulate real work** — right now `test-fixture` leaves `@` empty, so `finalize --squash` squashes nothing. Add a flag (say `--with-pending`) that writes some content to `@` in both repos so finalize has something to actually squash + push. Default off (cleanest fixture), enable when you want a more realistic demo.

3. **Cleanup subcommand** — new `vc-x1 test-fixture-rm PATH` (or `test-cleanup`) that `rm -rf`s the fixture, with a safety guard that the path matches the `vc-x1-test-*` naming convention AND sits inside `$TMPDIR` (or is passed to `--path`). Refuses otherwise, so it can't chew up arbitrary directories.

4. **Step markers in finalize output** — as described above, makes the mixed jj output easy to follow.

All four are small and would fold naturally into dev3. Scope creep risk is low. Thoughts / want me to proceed?

}

289	    debug!("finalize_exec: starting opts={opts:?}");
290	    let repo_str = opts.repo.to_string_lossy();
291	    let cwd = std::path::Path::new(".");
292

294	    if let Some(ref sq) = opts.squash {
295	        let delay = std::time::Duration::from_secs_f64(opts.delay_secs);

297	        std::thread::sleep(delay);
298	
299	        run(
300	            "jj",
301	            &[
302	                "squash",
303	                "--ignore-immutable",
304	                "--use-destination-message",
305	                "--from",
306	                &sq.source,
307	                "--into",
308	                &sq.target,
309	                "-R",
310	                &repo_str,
311	            ],
312	            cwd,
313	        )?;
314	    }
315	
316	    // Set bookmark and push if requested
317	    if let Some(ref bookmark) = opts.bookmark {
318	        // Verify bookmark exists — don't silently create new ones
319	        let result = run("jj", &["bookmark", "list", bookmark, "-R", &repo_str], cwd)?;
320	        if result.is_empty() {
321	            return Err(format!("bookmark '{bookmark}' does not exist").into());
322	        }
323	
324	        let rev = opts
325	            .squash
326	            .as_ref()
327	            .map(|sq| sq.target.as_str())
328	            .unwrap_or("@"); // OK: no squash spec → bookmark points at current @
329	        run(
330	            "jj",
331	            &["bookmark", "set", bookmark, "-r", rev, "-R", &repo_str],
332	            cwd,
333	        )?;
334

336	            run(
337	                "jj",
338	                &["git", "push", "--bookmark", bookmark, "-R", &repo_str],
339	                &opts.repo,
340	            )?;
341	        }
342	    }
343	
344	    debug!("finalize_exec: done");

  "new_string": "fn finalize_exec(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    debug!(\"finalize_exec: starting opts={opts:?}\");\n    let repo_str = opts.repo.to_string_lossy();\n    let cwd = std::path::Path::new(\".\");\n\n    // Squash if requested\n    if let Some(ref sq) = opts.squash {\n        let delay = std::time::Duration::from_secs_f64(opts.delay_secs);\n        debug!(\"finalize_exec: sleeping {delay:?}\");\n        std::thread::sleep(delay);\n\n        info!(\"finalize: squashing {} → {}...\", sq.source, sq.target);\n        run(\n            \"jj\",\n            &[\n                \"squash\",\n                \"--ignore-immutable\",\n                \"--use-destination-message\",\n                \"--from\",\n                &sq.source,\n                \"--into\",\n                &sq.target,\n                \"-R\",\n                &repo_str,\n            ],\n            cwd,\n        )?;\n    }\n\n    // Set bookmark and push if requested\n    if let Some(ref bookmark) = opts.bookmark {\n        // Verify bookmark exists — don't silently create new ones\n        let result = run(\"jj\", &[\"bookmark\", \"list\", bookmark, \"-R\", &repo_str], cwd)?;\n        if result.is_empty() {\n            return Err(format!(\"bookmark '{bookmark}' does not exist\").into());\n        }\n\n        let rev = opts\n            .squash\n            .as_ref()\n            .map(|sq| sq.target.as_str())\n            .unwrap_or(\"@\"); // OK: no squash spec → bookmark points at current @\n        info!(\"finalize: setting bookmark '{bookmark}' to {rev}...\");\n        run(\n            \"jj\",\n            &[\"bookmark\", \"set\", bookmark, \"-r\", rev, \"-R\", &repo_str],\n            cwd,\n        )?;\n\n        if opts.push {\n            info!(\"finalize: pushing '{bookmark}' to origin...\");\n            run(\n                \"jj\",\n                &[\"git\", \"push\", \"--bookmark\", bookmark, \"-R\", &repo_str],\n                &opts.repo,\n            )?;\n        }\n    }\n\n    info!(\"finalize: done\");\n    debug!(\"finalize_exec: done\");"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "### test-fixture\n\nScaffold a throwaway dual-repo jj workspace + local bare-git remotes\nfor testing `finalize` (and other subcommands) without touching live\nworkspace repos. Mirrors the real `vc-x1 init` layout minus the GitHub\nside and the `~/.claude/projects/` symlink. Both repos get a described\ninitial commit with matching `ochid:` trailers, a tracked `main`\nbookmark, and a pushed remote — so `finalize --push` flows work\nend-to-end on either side.",
  "new_string": "### test-fixture\n\nScaffold a throwaway dual-repo jj workspace + local bare-git remotes\nfor testing `finalize` (and other subcommands) without touching live\nworkspace repos. Mirrors the real `vc-x1 init` layout minus the GitHub\nside and the `~/.claude/projects/` symlink. Both repos get a described\ninitial commit with matching `ochid:` trailers, a tracked `main`\nbookmark, and a pushed remote — so `finalize --push` flows work\nend-to-end on either side.\n\n**Local remotes, not GitHub.** Each `origin` points at a bare-git\ndirectory alongside the work trees (`<base>/remote-code.git/`,\n`<base>/remote-claude.git/`) — no network, no auth, no GitHub. Pushes\nsucceed against these local bare repos and stay inside the fixture,\nso nothing leaks out and nothing needs cleanup on a remote service.\nWhen you're done, `vc-x1 test-fixture-rm <base>` wipes the whole\nthing."
}

Revised commit proposal:

```
jj commit -m \
"feat: /dev/tty, per-dev workflow, fixture refinements (0.33.0-dev3)" \
-m "finalize::detach() now wires the child's stdout/stderr explicitly.
Previously only stdin was nulled; inherited pipes to the bash-tool
caller got closed on parent exit, silently dropping child writes.

- Open /dev/tty read+write in the parent. If available, attach to
  the child's stdout/stderr so its output stays visible in the user's
  shell after the parent exits.
- Falls back to Stdio::null() when no controlling terminal (pipe,
  cron, Windows). Log file is authoritative there.

finalize_exec step markers — info! before each subprocess ('finalize:
squashing …', 'setting bookmark … to @-', 'pushing … to origin',
'done'). Makes the interleaved jj output unambiguous; previously
'Nothing changed.' from bookmark-set could be mistaken for push
failure.

CLAUDE.md — new 'Per-dev step workflow' subsection: each -devN
commit gets push + finalize with --detach --delay 10, then wait for
user to say 'continue'. Treats a -devN like a single commit shipped
to remote.

test-fixture refinements from hands-on testing:
- 'Try:' hint no longer suggests finalize on work/ (always plain
  jj git push); adds cleanup hint.
- --with-pending flag leaves uncommitted changes in @ (both repos)
  so finalize --squash has real work.
- New vc-x1 test-fixture-rm subcommand with a vc-x1-test- prefix
  safety guard.
- README 'Local remotes, not GitHub' callout; runtime output points
  at README § test-fixture.

README.md — captured real output blocks for test-fixture and
detached finalize (terminal view + log file view).

ochid: /.claude/ppvszwwwqqpw" \
-R .
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/src/finalize.rs",
  "old_string": "use std::path::PathBuf;\nuse std::process::Stdio;\n\nuse clap::Args;\nuse log::{debug, info};\n\nuse crate::common::run;",
  "new_string": "use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\nuse std::process::Stdio;\nuse std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};\n\nuse clap::Args;\nuse log::{debug, info};\n\nuse crate::common::run;\n\n/// Directory where the detached finalize child writes failure markers.\n/// A subsequent `vc-x1` invocation scans this directory and surfaces\n/// any failures to the user, closing the gap that the detached child's\n/// non-zero exit isn't observable by the caller.\nfn status_dir() -> Option<PathBuf> {\n    std::env::var_os(\"HOME\").map(|h| PathBuf::from(h).join(\".cache/vc-x1/finalize-status\"))\n}\n\nfn write_failure_marker(opts: &FinalizeOpts, err: &str) {\n    let Some(dir) = status_dir() else {\n        return;\n    };\n    if std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).is_err() {\n        return;\n    }\n    let ns = SystemTime::now()\n        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)\n        .map(|d| d.as_nanos())\n        .unwrap_or(0); // OK: filesystem-sortable timestamp; 0 on the impossible pre-epoch path\n    let pid = std::process::id();\n    let path = dir.join(format!(\"{ns}-{pid}.status\"));\n    let content = format!(\n        \"timestamp_ns={ns}\\npid={pid}\\nrepo={}\\nbookmark={}\\nerror={err}\\n\",\n        opts.repo.display(),\n        opts.bookmark.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"\"),\n    );\n    let _ = std::fs::write(&path, content);\n}\n\n/// Read, print, and delete any failure markers left by previous detached\n/// finalize children. Cheap no-op when the directory doesn't exist.\npub fn surface_previous_failures() {\n    let Some(dir) = status_dir() else {\n        return;\n    };\n    let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(&dir) else {\n        return;\n    };\n    let mut paths: Vec<PathBuf> = entries\n        .flatten()\n        .map(|e| e.path())\n        .filter(|p| p.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) == Some(\"status\"))\n        .collect();\n    paths.sort();\n    for path in paths {\n        if let Ok(content) = std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {\n            eprintln!(\n                \"warn: previous finalize failure ({}):\",\n                path.file_stem().map(|s| s.to_string_lossy()).unwrap_or_default() // OK: filename without extension; empty on unexpected absence\n            );\n            for line in content.lines() {\n                eprintln!(\"  {line}\");\n            }\n            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);\n        }\n    }\n}"
}

Users initially saw \"origin\" and assumed GitHub;\n  the docs + runtime pointer make the self-contained nature obvious.\n\n### 0.33.0-dev4 — status marker for post-detach failures\n\nThe detached finalize child's exit code isn't observable by the\ncaller (the parent already returned `0` before the child did\nanything meaningful). dev1's pre-flight catches most issues\nsynchronously, and dev3's `/dev/tty` reconnect gets the child's\noutput onto the user's terminal when one exists — but if the\nchild fails during its squash or push (network loss, remote\ndeleted, race), and the caller is pipe-invoked (bash tool, cron,\nCI), the failure would still be silent except in the `--log`\nfile.\n\ndev4 closes that gap with a **status marker file** that the child\nwrites on failure, and which any subsequent `vc-x1` invocation\nsurfaces to the user.\n\n**Writer** (`finalize::write_failure_marker`): when `finalize()`\nreturns `Err` and `opts.exec` is true (the detached child path),\nwrite one file per failure to `$HOME/.cache/vc-x1/finalize-status/`.\nFilename is `<ns-since-epoch>-<pid>.status`, sortable and unique.\nContent is simple `key=value` lines:\n```\ntimestamp_ns=…\npid=…\nrepo=…\nbookmark=…\nerror=<full error string>\n```\nOnly the detached-child path writes; the synchronous path's `Err`\nalready surfaces via `error!` through `main::run_command`.\n\n**Reader** (`finalize::surface_previous_failures`): on every\nnon-detached-child `vc-x1` invocation, called from `main()` right\nafter `Cli::parse()`. Scans the marker directory, prints each\nfailure to stderr with a `warn:` prefix, then deletes the file so\nit surfaces exactly once.\n\nSuppressed in the detached child (`--exec`) so a child doesn't\nconsume markers meant for the next interactive run. Detected via\n`matches!(cli.command, Commands::Finalize(ref f) if f.exec)` before\ndispatch.\n\n**Note: `--version` and `--help` short-circuit** inside `Cli::parse()`\n— clap exits before returning, so the surface step doesn't run for\nthose invocations. Acceptable: marker surfacing is already a\nbest-effort side channel, and the next actual subcommand\n(`vc-x1 chid`, `finalize`, etc.) picks them up.\n\nVerified end-to-end: created a fixture with `--with-pending`,\n`rm -rf`'d `remote-claude.git/objects` to break push, ran\n`vc-x1 finalize … --detach --delay 1`. Parent returned 0; child\nfailed push a second later; marker appeared under\n`~/.cache/vc-x1/finalize-status/`; the next `vc-x1 chid -R .` ran\nthe command normally after printing the failure context (including\nthe full `jj git push` error) to stderr; marker was gone\nafterwards."
}

Proposed app-repo commit:

```
jj commit -m \
"feat: status marker for post-detach finalize failures (0.33.0-dev4)" \
-m "The detached finalize child's exit code isn't observable by the
caller — the parent returned 0 before the child started real work.
Pre-flight (dev1) catches most issues up front, and /dev/tty
reconnect (dev3) delivers child output to interactive terminals,
but a pipe-invoked caller (bash tool, cron, CI) still sees nothing
when the child fails during its actual squash or push.

dev4 closes that gap with a best-effort status marker.

- finalize::write_failure_marker writes one file per failure under
  ~/.cache/vc-x1/finalize-status/ when finalize() returns Err in
  the --exec (detached-child) path. Content: key=value lines with
  timestamp, pid, repo, bookmark, and the full error.
- finalize::surface_previous_failures scans the directory on every
  vc-x1 invocation (called from main() after Cli::parse()), prints
  each marker to stderr with a 'warn: previous finalize failure …'
  header, then deletes the file so it surfaces exactly once.
- Suppressed in the detached child itself (--exec) so it can't
  consume markers meant for the next interactive run.

Verified end-to-end: sabotaged the remote, detached, child failed
push, next 'vc-x1 chid -R .' surfaced the full error context before
running normally.

ochid: /.claude/voxruntzzmmo" \
-R .
```

Approve?

> DEVELOPER

But the user will see any errors from the finalize in the terminal, correct? (I didn't see it mentioned in chores, may have missed it)

> AGENT

Correct — and I should make it explicit. The two mechanisms are complementary:

| Caller | Child output | Status marker |
|---|---|---|
| Interactive shell | Child writes to `/dev/tty` (dev3) — **visible live** in user's terminal after the delay | Also written (redundant but harmless; deleted on next invocation) |
| Pipe-invoked (Bash tool, cron, CI) | No tty → null → **invisible** | **Primary** visibility path — surfaced on next `vc-x1` invocation |

Dev3 is the happy path for interactive use; dev4 is the safety net for both interactive (if the user missed the live output) and the fully-silent pipe case. Let me add that to chores-04:

> TOOL

```
jj commit -m \
"feat: status marker for post-detach finalize failures (0.33.0-dev4)" \
-m "Closes the last gap in finalize-failure visibility. The detached
child's exit code isn't observable by the caller (the parent
returned 0 before the child started real work). dev1's pre-flight
catches most issues up front, dev3's /dev/tty reconnect delivers
child output live to interactive terminals — but a pipe-invoked
caller (bash tool, cron, CI) still sees nothing when the child
fails during its actual squash or push.

dev3 and dev4 are complementary, not alternatives:
- Interactive shells: /dev/tty shows live output; marker is a
  safety net if the user missed it.
- Pipe-invoked callers: no tty, no live output — marker is the
  primary visibility path.

Implementation:
- finalize::write_failure_marker writes one file per failure under
  ~/.cache/vc-x1/finalize-status/ when finalize() returns Err in
  the --exec (detached-child) path. Content: key=value lines with
  timestamp, pid, repo, bookmark, and the full error.

  vc-x1 invocation (called from main() after Cli::parse()), prints
  each marker to stderr with a 'warn:' header, then deletes the
  file so it surfaces exactly once.
- Suppressed in the detached child itself (--exec) so it can't
  consume markers meant for the next interactive run.

Verified end-to-end: sabotaged a fixture's remote, detached, child
failed push a second later, next 'vc-x1 chid -R .' surfaced the
full jj error context before running normally.

Parent returned 0; child\nfailed push a second later; marker appeared under\n`~/.cache/vc-x1/finalize-status/`; the next `vc-x1 chid -R .` ran\nthe command normally after printing the failure context (including\nthe full `jj git push` error) to stderr; marker was gone\nafterwards.\n\n### 0.33.0-dev4.1 — complete `test-fixture` \"Try:\" flow\n\nThe dev2 \"Try:\" hint was too terse — it skipped the `jj bookmark\nset` step needed to advance `main` after `jj describe @`, so a user\nfollowing the hint verbatim got `Warning: No bookmarks found in the\ndefault push revset` and no push happened.\n\nExpanded into a three-step end-to-end recipe that actually works:\n\n```\n# 1. code repo: described commit → advance main → push\necho hello > <work>/hello.txt\njj describe @ -R <work> -m 'feat: add hello.txt'\njj bookmark set main -r @ -R <work>\njj git push -R <work>\n\n# 2. session repo: trailing writes → finalize (squash into @-, push)\necho notes > <work>/.claude/notes.md\nvc-x1 finalize --repo <work>/.claude --squash --push main --detach\n\n# 3. cleanup\nvc-x1 test-fixture-rm <base>\n```\n\nSame output appears in test-fixture's runtime log *and* the captured\nexample in README.md § test-fixture, so users see the identical flow\nin either place."

{

  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/README.md",
  "old_string": "### Testing finalize\n\nAlways test against a throwaway fixture, never the live workspace.\nUse `test-fixture` (above) to scaffold one per test:\n\n```bash\n# Create fixture and capture paths\nbase=$(mktemp -u /tmp/vc-x1-test-XXXXXX)\nvc-x1 test-fixture --path \"$base\"\nwork=\"$base/work\"\nsession=\"$base/work/.claude\"\n\n# Foreground (default, blocks, log to fixture)\nvc-x1 finalize --repo \"$work\" --push main --log \"$work/finalize.log\"\ncat \"$work/finalize.log\"\n\n# Detached (returns immediately, child logs to fixture)\nbase2=$(mktemp -u /tmp/vc-x1-test-XXXXXX)\nvc-x1 test-fixture --path \"$base2\"\nsession2=\"$base2/work/.claude\"\nvc-x1 finalize --detach --repo \"$session2\" --squash --push main \\\n    --log \"$session2/finalize.log\"\ncat \"$session2/finalize.log\"\n```\n\nThe log file shows timestamped (nanoseconds) entries with PIDs, covering\nthe full flow: `main` entry/exit, `finalize` entry/exit, `detach`\nspawn, and `finalize_exec` in the child process.\n\nExample — detached finalize against a fresh fixture. What the user\nsees in the terminal (the parent process) is the pre-flight plan and\nthe detach confirmation; the child's work continues in the background:",
  "new_string": "### Testing push + finalize\n\nAlways test against a throwaway fixture, never the live workspace.\nScaffold one with `test-fixture` (above), then run the complete\npush + finalize flow end-to-end. The code repo uses plain\n`jj git push`; the session repo uses `vc-x1 finalize` to squash\ntrailing writes and push in one shot.\n\n```bash\nbase=$(mktemp -u /tmp/vc-x1-test-XXXXXX)\nvc-x1 test-fixture --path \"$base\"\nwork=\"$base/work\"\nsession=\"$base/work/.claude\"\n\n# 1. code repo: described commit → advance main → push\necho hello > \"$work/hello.txt\"\njj describe @ -R \"$work\" -m 'feat: add hello.txt'\njj bookmark set main -r @ -R \"$work\"\njj git push -R \"$work\"\n\n# 2. session repo: trailing writes → finalize (squash into @-, push)\necho notes > \"$session/notes.md\"\nvc-x1 finalize --repo \"$session\" --squash --push main --detach \\\n    --log \"$session/finalize.log\"\n\n# 3. inspect the detached child's log once it's done (≈10s by default)\nsleep 12 && cat \"$session/finalize.log\"\n\n# 4. cleanup when done\nvc-x1 test-fixture-rm \"$base\"\n```\n\n**Why `jj git push` for code but `finalize` for `.claude`?** The\ncode repo's workflow is a plain dev commit on `@-` that we push\ndirectly. The session repo mirrors the bot's runtime pattern:\nsession writes land in `@` (above the last committed dev commit),\nand `finalize --squash @,@-` folds those trailing writes into the\ndev commit just before pushing, so one atomic state goes upstream.\n\nThe log file shows timestamped (nanoseconds) entries with PIDs,\ncovering the full flow: `main` entry/exit, `finalize` entry/exit,\n`detach` spawn, and `finalize_exec` in the child process.\n\nExample — detached finalize against a fresh fixture. What the user\nsees in the terminal (the parent process) is the pre-flight plan and\nthe detach confirmation; the child's work continues in the background:"
}
```

Old section\nshowed `vc-x1 finalize --repo <work> --push main` (no `--squash`),\nwhich the dev1.1 preflight rightly rejects because `@` has no\ndescription — a footgun directly in the docs. New section runs the\nreal push-for-code, finalize-for-session pattern end-to-end and\nexplains why the two repos get different tooling. TOC updated.\n\n### 0.33.0 — release rollup\n\nLayered visibility for `finalize` failures. Each dev step closed a\ndistinct gap; together they mean a failed finalize is never silent\nregardless of how it was invoked:\n\n| dev   | What it adds                                            | Failure caught / surfaced                                           |\n| ----- | ------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| dev1  | Synchronous pre-flight in the parent (`preflight()`)    | Bad bookmark, non-tracking remote, unresolved squash revset         |\n| dev1.1| Conflict / forward-only / push-target-description checks + plan logging; CLAUDE.md post-commit bookmark-advance workflow | Conflicts, divergent bookmark move, undescribed push target         |\n| dev2  | `common::run()` stderr at `info!`; `test-fixture` dual-repo scaffold | jj's own messages (e.g. `Nothing changed.`, push summaries) visible without `-v` |\n| dev3  | `/dev/tty` reconnect for detached child; step markers in `finalize_exec`; CLAUDE.md per-dev push+finalize workflow; `test-fixture` refinements (`--with-pending`, `test-fixture-rm`, hint fixes, README local-remotes callout) | Interactive terminal sees child output live after detach; per-dev `-devN` is pushed + finalized, so intermediate work isn't lost |\n| dev4  | Status marker in `~/.cache/vc-x1/finalize-status/`; `surface_previous_failures` at every `vc-x1` startup | Pipe-invoked callers (bash tool, cron, CI) get their previous child's failure on the next run |\n| dev4.1| README \"Testing push + finalize\" rewrite with complete flow; runtime breadcrumb in test-fixture Try | Users following the docs actually succeed at push+finalize end-to-end |\n\nEnd state: for a detached finalize,\n- synchronous issues never reach detach (exit non-zero, error on stderr),\n- child output lands on the user's terminal when `/dev/tty` exists,\n- child output is captured in the `--log` file regardless,\n- any child-side failure leaves a marker that the next invocation surfaces,\n- per-dev push+finalize hygiene means nothing sits unpushed at a\n  `-devN` commit."
}

> AGENT

All checks pass. Proposed 0.33.0 release commit:

```
jj commit -m \
"feat: finalize-failure visibility rollup (0.33.0)" \
-m "Ships the multi-dev series that makes finalize failures visible
regardless of how the command was invoked. Prior behavior: a
detached child that failed mid-run was effectively silent — the
parent returned 0 before the child started real work, and the
child's stderr either never reached the user (inherited pipes
closed on parent exit) or only landed in the --log file which
nobody read.

Layered fix across dev1 → dev4.1 (details in notes/chores-04.md):

- dev1: synchronous preflight in the parent (bookmark existence,
  non-tracking remote, squash revset resolution).
- dev1.1: conflict / forward-only / push-target-description checks
  + plan logging; CLAUDE.md post-commit bookmark-advance workflow.
- dev2: common::run() subprocess stderr at info! (so jj's own
  messages are visible without -v); vc-x1 test-fixture dual-repo
  scaffold.
- dev3: /dev/tty reconnect for the detached child (output on the
  user's terminal when one exists); step markers in finalize_exec;
  CLAUDE.md per-dev push+finalize workflow; test-fixture refinements
  (--with-pending, test-fixture-rm cleanup, hint fixes, README
  local-remotes callout).
- dev4: status marker file under ~/.cache/vc-x1/finalize-status/;
  surface_previous_failures() at every vc-x1 startup — pipe-invoked
  callers (bash tool, cron, CI) get their previous child's failure
  on the next run.
- dev4.1: README 'Testing push + finalize' rewrite; test-fixture
  runtime output is a short breadcrumb to the README.

notes/todo.md: entry moved from In Progress to Done. Version
rolled from 0.33.0-dev4.1 to 0.33.0.

ochid: /.claude/nulqmslmvwkn" \
-R .
```
